D. **Cytosol**: Cytosolic proteins lack signal peptides and transmembrane domains. The presence of both a signal peptide and a transmembrane domain rules out cytosolic localization.

Pannexin 1 is a ~466 amino acid protein with 4 transmembrane domains that forms hexameric channels (pannexons) in the plasma membrane. These channels are permeable to molecules &lt;1 kDa, including ions such as Ca²⁺ and nucleotides such as ATP. This matches option A perfectly.
